```ts
import { CountryTag } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/Countries";
import { GeneralTrait, traitBit } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/LandTypes";
import { dayOfDate } from "./Calendar";

export interface GeneralDef {
  /** Translation key ww1936.general.<id>. */
  id: string;
  tag: CountryTag;
  att: number;
  def: number;
  plan: number;
  log: number;
  traits: readonly GeneralTrait[];
  /** ISO date it can first serve (spec §6.2); none: from the start. */
  from?: string;
  /** ISO date it leaves (arrest, dismissal). */
  until?: string;
  /** Spans it cannot serve, [from, until) ISO. */
  gaps?: readonly (readonly [string, string])[];
  /** Where the dates and the exclusion check come from (Task 10). */
  source: string;
}

// Append only: a general's id in a game is its index + 1 (plan decision 17).
// Task 10 fills the roster (spec §6.2); these two are the foundation's test seed.
export const GENERALS: readonly GeneralDef[] = [
  { id: "rundstedt", tag: "DEU", att: 4, def: 4, plan: 3, log: 3, traits: [], source: "seed" },
  { id: "bock", tag: "DEU", att: 4, def: 3, plan: 3, log: 3, traits: [], source: "seed" },
];

export function traitMask(traits: readonly GeneralTrait[]): number {
  let m = 0;
  for (const t of traits) m |= traitBit(t);
  return m;
}

/** GENERALS ids of a country, by skill sum descending then id. */
export function rosterOf(tag: CountryTag): number[] {
  const out: number[] = [];
  GENERALS.forEach((g, i) => {
    if (g.tag === tag) out.push(i + 1);
  });
  const sum = (id: number) => {
    const g = GENERALS[id - 1];
    return g.att + g.def + g.plan + g.log;
  };
  return out.sort((a, b) => sum(b) - sum(a) || a - b);
}

/** Spec §6.2: whether the general can serve on `day`. */
export function generalServes(g: GeneralDef, day: number): boolean {
  if (g.from !== undefined && day < dayOfDate(g.from)) return false;
  if (g.until !== undefined && day >= dayOfDate(g.until)) return false;
  for (const [a, b] of g.gaps ?? []) {
    if (day >= dayOfDate(a) && day < dayOfDate(b)) return false;
  }
  return true;
}
```

```ts
import { CountryTag } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/Countries";
import { WwTechId } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/EconomyTypes";
import { BattalionId, SupportId, TemplateDef } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/LandTypes";

export interface StartTemplate {
  /** ww1936.tpl.<key>. */
  key: string;
  def: TemplateDef;
}

const tpl = (key: string, bns: [BattalionId, number][], support: SupportId[] = []): StartTemplate => ({
  key,
  def: { name: "", battalions: bns.flatMap(([id, n]) => new Array<BattalionId>(n).fill(id)), support },
});

/** Spec §4.5. The first template is the one the generic order of battle uses. */
export function startTemplates(tag: CountryTag, dev: number): StartTemplate[] {
  switch (tag) {
    case "DEU":
      return [
        tpl("infantry", [["infantry", 9], ["artillery", 2]], ["engineer", "recon"]),
        tpl("armored", [["light_armor", 4], ["motorized", 2], ["mot_artillery", 1]], ["recon"]),
        tpl("mountain", [["mountain", 6], ["artillery", 1]]),
        tpl("cavalry", [["cavalry", 4]]),
      ];
    case "ITA":
      return [
        tpl("binary", [["infantry", 6], ["artillery", 2]], ["engineer"]),
        tpl("alpini", [["mountain", 6], ["artillery", 1]]),
        tpl("celere", [["light_armor", 2], ["cavalry", 2], ["mot_artillery", 1]]),
        tpl("colonial", [["infantry", 6]]),
      ];
    case "JPN":
      return [
        tpl("square", [["infantry", 12], ["artillery", 2]], ["engineer"]),
        tpl("cavalry_brigade", [["cavalry", 3]]),
        tpl("garrison", [["infantry", 6]]),
      ];
    case "GBR":
      return [
        tpl("infantry", [["infantry", 9], ["artillery", 3]], ["engineer", "recon"]),
        tpl("mobile", [["light_armor", 3], ["motorized", 2], ["mot_artillery", 1]]),
        tpl("colonial_garrison", [["infantry", 6]]),
      ];
    case "FRA":
      return [
        tpl("infantry", [["infantry", 9], ["artillery", 3]], ["engineer"]),
        tpl("dlm", [["light_armor", 2], ["motorized", 3], ["mot_artillery", 1]], ["recon"]),
        tpl("colonial", [["infantry", 6]]),
        tpl("cavalry", [["cavalry", 4]]),
      ];
    case "USA":
      return [
        tpl("square", [["infantry", 9], ["artillery", 3]], ["engineer"]),
        tpl("cavalry", [["cavalry", 4]]),
        tpl("garrison", [["infantry", 6]]),
      ];
    case "SUN":
      return [
        tpl("rifle", [["infantry", 9], ["artillery", 2]]),
        tpl("cavalry", [["cavalry", 4], ["light_armor", 1]]),
        tpl("tank_brigade", [["light_armor", 3], ["motorized", 1]]),
        tpl("mountain_rifle", [["mountain", 6]]),
      ];
    default: {
      const out = [tpl("infantry", dev >= 3 ? [["infantry", 9], ["artillery", 1]] : [["infantry", 9]])];
      if (dev <= 2) out.push(tpl("cavalry", [["cavalry", 4]]));
      return out;
    }
  }
}

/** Spec §4.6: the land techs a country starts with. */
export function landStartTechs(tag: CountryTag, major: boolean, dev: number): WwTechId[] {
  const out: WwTechId[] = [];
  if (major) out.push("engineer_company", "recon_company");
  else if (dev >= 3) out.push("engineer_company");
  if (tag === "DEU" || tag === "ITA") out.push("mountaineers");
  if (tag === "GBR" || tag === "USA" || tag === "JPN") out.push("marines");
  if (tag === "FRA") out.push("fortification_1");
  return out;
}

export interface OobArmy {
  /** Region ids, first one the country controls at the start wins (Task 10). */
  regions: readonly number[];
  /** [template key, divisions]. */
  divisions: readonly (readonly [string, number])[];
  /** An operation already running (Italy in Ethiopia, spec §6.4). */
  planTargets?: readonly number[];
}

// Spec §6.4 majors; used only when scenario.json says historicalOob (plan
// decision 14). Task 10 fills it.
export const START_OOB: Readonly<Partial<Record<CountryTag, readonly OobArmy[]>>> = {};

/** Spec §6.4 "그 밖": min(50, startArmy × r / men), r by development. */
export function genericDivisions(startArmy: number, dev: number, divisionMen: number): number {
  const pct = dev >= 3 ? 100 : dev === 2 ? 50 : 30;
  return Math.max(0, Math.min(50, Math.floor((startArmy * pct) / 100 / Math.max(1, divisionMen))));
}
```

  교리 효과를 결정 11대로 바꾼다(`mobile_1`은 `speedPct: 10` 그대로).
- `EconomyRules.ts`: 결정 12의 함수를 지운다. `projectSpeedPct`에 `+ (i.building === "fort" ? i.tech.fortConstructionPct : 0) + (i.building === "rail" ? i.tech.railConstructionPct : 0)`.
- `EconomyChecks.ts`: `queuedInRegion`이 `{ factories, infra, fort, rail, supply_hub }`를 돌려준다(단계 건물은 이름별, 나머지는 `factories`). `BuildCheck.infraWithQueue`를 `levelWithQueue`("그 단계 건물의 지금 단계 + 대기열이 더할 단계")로 바꾸고 `buildBlockOf`는:

```ts
export function buildBlockOf(c: BuildCheck): WwOrderBlock | null {
  if (!BUILDABLE.includes(c.building)) return "not_buildable";
  if (!c.controlled) return "not_controlled";
  if (c.queueLength >= WW_MAX_QUEUE) return "queue_full";
  const max = LEVEL_MAX[c.building];
  // Level buildings use no slot and go up in occupied regions too (P2 §11.7).
  if (max !== undefined) return c.levelWithQueue + c.count > max ? "infra_max" : null;
  if (c.occupied) return "occupied";
  return c.usedSlots + c.count > c.slots ? "no_slots" : null;
}
```

  `infra_max` 문구는 Task 9가 "최대 단계"로 넓힌다.
- `Equipment.ts`: `EquipmentDef.bonus`와 그 값들을 지운다.
- `ModeConfig.ts`: `WW1936_DISABLED_UNITS`에 `UnitType.TransportShip`, `UnitType.DefensePost`, `UnitType.TradeShip`(결정 7).
- `EconomyStart.ts`: 그대로(`startArmy`·`startStock`은 `economy.start`가 계속 쓴다).

- [ ] **Step 12: 지상전 상태 `packages/engine/src/ww1936/land/WwLand.ts`** (spec §16, 결정 1, 9, 15, 16, 21)

```ts
// World War 1936 P2 — the land war (docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-20-ww1936-p2-divisions-design.md).
// One WwLand lives in WorldWar next to the economy; WorldWarExecution
// ticks it after economy.tick. Every list iterates in number order.
import { MessageType, PlayerType } from "@openfront/engine-api/game/GameTypes";
import type { WwOrder } from "@openfront/engine-api/Schemas";
import { CountryTag, FACTION_IDS } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/Countries";
import { WW_DAY_SPLIT, WwOrderBlock } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/EconomyTypes";
import {
  ARMY_AUTO, ARMY_BATTLE, ARMY_ENCIRCLED, ARMY_EXECUTING, ARMY_MOVING, ARMY_PARA, ARMY_RAIL,
  ARMY_RETREAT, ARMY_SEA, ArmyOrder, ArmySummary, BattleSummary, CountryLandDetail,
  CountryLandSummary, DivisionStats, GROUND_SLOTS, idleOrder, MOVE_NONE, MOVE_RAIL,
  MOVE_RETREAT, MOVE_SEA, RegionLandData, STANCES, Stance, TemplateDef, WW_MAX_ARMIES,
  WwLandData,
} from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/LandTypes";
import { zInt } from "@openfront/engine-lib/snapshot/SnapshotType";
import { dayStartTick } from "@openfront/engine-lib/ww1936/Calendar";
import { addToSide, emptySide } from "@openfront/engine-lib/ww1936/CombatRules";
import {
  divisionStats, genMixOf, strengthPm, templateStats,
} from "@openfront/engine-lib/ww1936/TemplateStats";
import { z } from "zod";
import type { Game, Player } from "../../game/Game";
import type { WwEconomy } from "../economy/WwEconomy";
import type { WorldWar } from "../WorldWar";
import { aiLandTick } from "./AiLand";
import { dailyEntrench, stepBattles, stepRecovery } from "./Battles";
import { dailyFronts } from "./Fronts";
import { appointGeneral, assignGeneral } from "./GeneralsRun";
import { startLand } from "./LandStartRun";
import { stepMovement } from "./Movement";
import { stepSpread } from "./Occupation";
import { applyArmyOrders, issueArmyOrder } from "./Orders";
import { dailySupply } from "./Supply";
import { dailySustain } from "./Sustain";
import { cancelTraining, deleteTemplate, deployNow, queueTraining, saveTemplate } from "./Training";

// ---- Divisions: one row each, columns (spec §5.2) ----

const COLS = {
  id: Int32Array,
  owner: Uint16Array,
  template: Uint16Array,
  army: Int32Array,
  region: Uint16Array,
  next: Uint16Array,
  edge: Int32Array,
  prog: Int32Array,
  need: Int32Array,
  /** Milli-km (rail, walk) or milli-ticks (sea) the last tick moved; the screens interpolate with it. */
  step: Int32Array,
  mode: Uint8Array,
  dest: Uint16Array,
  men: Int32Array,
  org: Int32Array,
  exp: Int16Array,
  entrench: Uint8Array,
  stillDays: Uint16Array,
  supply: Int16Array,
  encircled: Uint8Array,
  battle: Uint16Array,
  role: Uint8Array,
  front: Uint8Array,
  lossMen: Int32Array,
  /** Last day it fought (experience, generals, army XP); -1: never. */
  lastFight: Int32Array,
} as const;
export type DivisionColumn = keyof typeof COLS;
export const DIVISION_COLUMNS = Object.keys(COLS) as DivisionColumn[];
type IntArray = Int32Array | Uint16Array | Uint8Array | Int16Array;

/**
 * Spec §5.2: the divisions as columns. Rows stay in division-id order:
 * ids only rise, a removed row is a tombstone (id 0) until the daily
 * compaction (plan decision 15). Row numbers are valid within one tick;
 * keep division ids across ticks.
 */
export class DivisionTable {
  rows = 0;
  id = new Int32Array(0);
  owner = new Uint16Array(0);
  template = new Uint16Array(0);
  army = new Int32Array(0);
  region = new Uint16Array(0);
  next = new Uint16Array(0);
  edge = new Int32Array(0);
  prog = new Int32Array(0);
  need = new Int32Array(0);
  step = new Int32Array(0);
  mode = new Uint8Array(0);
  dest = new Uint16Array(0);
  men = new Int32Array(0);
  org = new Int32Array(0);
  exp = new Int16Array(0);
  entrench = new Uint8Array(0);
  stillDays = new Uint16Array(0);
  supply = new Int16Array(0);
  encircled = new Uint8Array(0);
  battle = new Uint16Array(0);
  role = new Uint8Array(0);
  front = new Uint8Array(0);
  lossMen = new Int32Array(0);
  lastFight = new Int32Array(0);
  /** GROUND_SLOTS per row, GROUND_EQUIP_KEYS order. */
  equip = new Int32Array(0);
  /** Milli-units of equipment lost but not yet taken (spec §5.5). */
  lossEq = new Int32Array(0);
  /** Derived (DERIVED_FIELDS): division id → row. */
  rowIndex = new Map<number, number>();
  private cap = 0;

  constructor() {
    // Always at least 64 rows, so an empty table and its restore are equal.
    this.grow(64);
  }

  col(name: DivisionColumn): IntArray {
    return (this as unknown as Record<DivisionColumn, IntArray>)[name];
  }

  private grow(min: number): void {
    const cap = Math.max(64, this.cap * 2, min);
    for (const name of DIVISION_COLUMNS) {
      const next = new COLS[name](cap);
      next.set(this.col(name).subarray(0, this.rows));
      (this as unknown as Record<DivisionColumn, IntArray>)[name] = next;
    }
    for (const name of ["equip", "lossEq"] as const) {
      const next = new Int32Array(cap * GROUND_SLOTS);
      next.set(this[name].subarray(0, this.rows * GROUND_SLOTS));
      this[name] = next;
    }
    this.cap = cap;
  }

  /** Appends a division; ids rise, so the rows stay in id order. */
  add(id: number, owner: number, template: number, army: number, region: number): number {
    if (this.rows === this.cap) this.grow(this.rows + 1);
    const r = this.rows++;
    for (const name of DIVISION_COLUMNS) this.col(name)[r] = 0;
    this.equip.fill(0, r * GROUND_SLOTS, (r + 1) * GROUND_SLOTS);
    this.lossEq.fill(0, r * GROUND_SLOTS, (r + 1) * GROUND_SLOTS);
    this.id[r] = id;
    this.owner[r] = owner;
    this.template[r] = template;
    this.army[r] = army;
    this.region[r] = region;
    this.dest[r] = region;
    this.edge[r] = -1;
    this.supply[r] = 1000;
    this.lastFight[r] = -1;
    this.rowIndex.set(id, r);
    return r;
  }

  alive(r: number): boolean {
    return this.id[r] !== 0;
  }

  rowOf(id: number): number {
    return this.rowIndex.get(id) ?? -1;
  }

  count(): number {
    return this.rowIndex.size;
  }

  /** A tombstone (an all-zero row) until compact(). */
  kill(r: number): void {
    this.rowIndex.delete(this.id[r]);
    for (const name of DIVISION_COLUMNS) this.col(name)[r] = 0;
    this.equip.fill(0, r * GROUND_SLOTS, (r + 1) * GROUND_SLOTS);
    this.lossEq.fill(0, r * GROUND_SLOTS, (r + 1) * GROUND_SLOTS);
  }

  equipOf(r: number): Int32Array {
    return this.equip.subarray(r * GROUND_SLOTS, (r + 1) * GROUND_SLOTS);
  }

  /** Drops tombstones, keeping the order. True when rows moved. */
  compact(): boolean {
    let w = 0;
    for (let r = 0; r < this.rows; r++) {
      if (this.id[r] === 0) continue;
      if (w !== r) {
        for (const name of DIVISION_COLUMNS) this.col(name)[w] = this.col(name)[r];
        this.equip.copyWithin(w * GROUND_SLOTS, r * GROUND_SLOTS, (r + 1) * GROUND_SLOTS);
        this.lossEq.copyWithin(w * GROUND_SLOTS, r * GROUND_SLOTS, (r + 1) * GROUND_SLOTS);
      }
      w++;
    }
    const moved = w !== this.rows;
    // The tail is zeros again, so a restored table is equal element by element.
    for (const name of DIVISION_COLUMNS) this.col(name).fill(0, w, this.rows);
    this.equip.fill(0, w * GROUND_SLOTS, this.rows * GROUND_SLOTS);
    this.lossEq.fill(0, w * GROUND_SLOTS, this.rows * GROUND_SLOTS);
    this.rows = w;
    this.rebuildIndex();
    return moved;
  }

  rebuildIndex(): void {
    this.rowIndex = new Map();
    for (let r = 0; r < this.rows; r++) if (this.id[r] !== 0) this.rowIndex.set(this.id[r], r);
  }

  /**
   * Every used row (tombstones are zero rows, at most a day's worth) and the
   * capacity, so a restore rebuilds the very same arrays (the snapshot
   * oracles compare them element by element). Never compacts: a snapshot
   * must not change the game.
   */
  snapshot(): DivisionTableState {
    const cols = {} as Record<DivisionColumn, number[]>;
    for (const name of DIVISION_COLUMNS) cols[name] = Array.from(this.col(name).subarray(0, this.rows));
    return {
      ...cols,
      cap: this.cap,
      equip: Array.from(this.equip.subarray(0, this.rows * GROUND_SLOTS)),
      lossEq: Array.from(this.lossEq.subarray(0, this.rows * GROUND_SLOTS)),
    };
  }

  restore(s: DivisionTableState): void {
    const n = s.id.length;
    this.rows = 0;
    this.cap = 0;
    this.grow(s.cap);
    for (const name of DIVISION_COLUMNS) this.col(name).set(s[name]);
    this.equip.set(s.equip);
    this.lossEq.set(s.lossEq);
    this.rows = n;
    this.rebuildIndex();
  }
}

// ---- Armies, battles, spreads, countries ----

export interface Army {
  id: number;
  owner: number;
  /** "N-th army" (spec §18). */
  nameNo: number;
  /** General id (plan decision 17); 0: none. */
  general: number;
  /** Division ids, ascending. */
  divisions: number[];
  order: ArmyOrder;
  auto: boolean;
  planBp: number;
  /** Front regions of the last daily recompute (spec §8.1), in BFS order. */
  front: number[];
  /** Remaining regions of a move order's path. */
  path: number[];
  /** Its order changed: applyArmyOrders turns it into destinations this tick. */
  pending: boolean;
  /** Day of the last paradrop; -1: never. */
  paraLastDay: number;
}

export interface Battle {
  /** The defending region; also the battle's number. */
  region: number;
  sinceTick: number;
  round: number;
  /** Division ids, ascending. */
  attackers: number[];
  defenders: number[];
  /** Ids in line this round, in line order (spec §9.2). */
  attLine: number[];
  defLine: number[];
  /** Defender org at the start plus every newcomer's (spec §12.3), milli. */
  startOrgMilli: number;
  lostOrgMilli: number;
  /** Colour of the spread (spec §9.1): widest attacker country, defender = controller. */
  attLead: number;
  defLead: number;
}

export const SPREAD_BATTLE = 0;
export const SPREAD_CAPTURE = 1;
export const SPREAD_REVERT = 2;
export const SPREAD_CAPITULATION = 3;

/** Spec §12: tiles of `region` going to `by`. The tile order is derived from `sources`. */
export interface Spread {
  region: number;
  by: number;
  kind: number;
  /** Regions the spread starts from (attackers' regions); empty: from the center. */
  sources: number[];
  /** Tiles of the order already given to `by`. */
  done: number;
  /** `done` at the start of `dayNo` (the 20%-a-day cap). */
  dayBase: number;
  dayNo: number;
}

export interface LandTemplate {
  id: number;
  /** ww1936.tpl.<key> for a 1936 default; "" for a player's own. */
  key: string;
  def: TemplateDef;
}

export interface TrainingEntry {
  id: number;
  template: number;
  /** Divisions not started yet. */
  remaining: number;
  /** 0: a new army. */
  army: number;
  /** 0: the capital region. */
  region: number;
}

export interface Trainee {
  entry: number;
  template: number;
  men: number;
  /** GROUND_SLOTS. */
  equip: number[];
  days: number;
}

export interface GeneralState {
  id: number;
  /** GENERALS index; -1 for a titled general. */
  roster: number;
  titleNo: number;
  att: number;
  dfn: number;
  plan: number;
  log: number;
  traits: number;
  xp: number;
  level: number;
  /** Army id; 0: free. */
  army: number;
  /** Days spent attacking, defending, preparing, short of supply (spec §6.2). */
  use: number[];
  /** Battle days in winter, desert, mountains, across rivers (trait unlocks). */
  terrainDays: number[];
}

export class CountryLand {
  templates: LandTemplate[] = [];
  nextTemplateId = 1;
  training: TrainingEntry[] = [];
  trainees: Trainee[] = [];
  nextEntryId = 1;
  armyXpMilli = 0;
  generals: GeneralState[] = [];
  nextArmyNo = 1;
  nextTitleNo = 1;
  casualtiesToday = 0;
  readyToday = 0;
  warnedTileAttack = false;

  constructor(
    readonly smallID: number,
    readonly tag: CountryTag,
  ) {}

  template(id: number): LandTemplate | null {
    return this.templates.find((t) => t.id === id) ?? null;
  }
}

// ---- Snapshot schema (spec §16.5) ----

const zInts = () => z.array(zInt());
const DivisionTableStateSchema = z.object({
  ...(Object.fromEntries(DIVISION_COLUMNS.map((c) => [c, zInts()])) as Record<DivisionColumn, ReturnType<typeof zInts>>),
  cap: zInt(),
  equip: zInts(),
  lossEq: zInts(),
});
type DivisionTableState = z.infer<typeof DivisionTableStateSchema>;

const OrderSchema = z.object({
  kind: z.enum(["idle", "move", "front", "plan", "paradrop"]),
  to: zInt(),
  mode: zInt(),
  anchor: zInt(),
  enemy: zInt(),
  targets: zInts(),
  stance: z.enum(STANCES),
  executing: z.boolean(),
  readyDay: zInt(),
});

export const WwLandStateSchema = z.object({
  controller: zInts(),
  supplyMilli: zInts(),
  encircled: zInts(),
  lastCommonDay: zInt(),
  nextDivisionId: zInt(),
  nextArmyId: zInt(),
  controlChanges: zInt(),
  divisions: DivisionTableStateSchema,
  armies: z.array(
    z.object({
      id: zInt(), owner: zInt(), nameNo: zInt(), general: zInt(), divisions: zInts(),
      order: OrderSchema, auto: z.boolean(), planBp: zInt(), front: zInts(), path: zInts(),
      pending: z.boolean(), paraLastDay: zInt(),
    }),
  ),
  battles: z.array(
    z.object({
      region: zInt(), sinceTick: zInt(), round: zInt(), attackers: zInts(), defenders: zInts(),
      attLine: zInts(), defLine: zInts(), startOrgMilli: zInt(), lostOrgMilli: zInt(),
      attLead: zInt(), defLead: zInt(),
    }),
  ),
  spreads: z.array(
    z.object({
      region: zInt(), by: zInt(), kind: zInt(), sources: zInts(), done: zInt(),
      dayBase: zInt(), dayNo: zInt(),
    }),
  ),
  countries: z.array(
    z.object({
      smallID: zInt(),
      templates: z.array(z.object({
        id: zInt(), key: z.string(), name: z.string(),
        battalions: z.array(z.string()), support: z.array(z.string()),
      })),
      nextTemplateId: zInt(),
      training: z.array(z.object({ id: zInt(), template: zInt(), remaining: zInt(), army: zInt(), region: zInt() })),
      trainees: z.array(z.object({ entry: zInt(), template: zInt(), men: zInt(), equip: zInts(), days: zInt() })),
      nextEntryId: zInt(),
      armyXpMilli: zInt(),
      generals: z.array(z.object({
        id: zInt(), roster: zInt(), titleNo: zInt(), att: zInt(), dfn: zInt(), plan: zInt(),
        log: zInt(), traits: zInt(), xp: zInt(), level: zInt(), army: zInt(), use: zInts(),
        terrainDays: zInts(),
      })),
      nextArmyNo: zInt(),
      nextTitleNo: zInt(),
      casualtiesToday: zInt(),
      readyToday: zInt(),
      warnedTileAttack: z.boolean(),
    }),
  ),
});
export type WwLandState = z.infer<typeof WwLandStateSchema>;

interface Side {
  key: number;
  /** Smallest member smallID: the side's supply runs on its turn (spec §2.2). */
  rep: number;
  members: number[];
}

interface StatsEntry {
  base: DivisionStats;
  eff: DivisionStats;
}

export class WwLand {
  readonly controller: Uint16Array;
  /** Supply of each region for its controller's side, milli (spec §11.2, decision 16). */
  readonly supplyMilli: Int32Array;
  /** 1: cut off from every hub of its controller's side (spec §11.5). */
  readonly encircled: Uint8Array;
  readonly divisions = new DivisionTable();
  readonly armies = new Map<number, Army>();
  readonly battles = new Map<number, Battle>();
  readonly spreads = new Map<number, Spread>();
  nextDivisionId = 1;
  nextArmyId = 1;
  controlChanges = 0;
  private lastCommonDay = -1;
  private countryList: (CountryLand | null)[];
  // Derived (DERIVED_FIELDS).
  private sideOfId: number[] = [];
  private sideListCache: Side[] = [];
  private statsCache = new Map<number, StatsEntry>();
  private tplCache = new Map<string, DivisionStats>();
  private regionRows: number[][] = [];
  private regionRowsDirty = true;
  /** Spread tile orders (rebuilt from Spread.sources), Task 4. */
  readonly spreadOrders = new Map<number, Uint32Array>();
  // Screens.
  private dirtyArmies = new Set<number>();
  private dirtyBattles = new Set<number>();
  private dirtyLandRegions = new Set<number>();
  private dirtyLandCountries = new Set<number>();
  private removedArmyIds: number[] = [];
  private endedBattleIds: number[] = [];

  constructor(
    readonly mg: Game,
    readonly ww: WorldWar,
    readonly econ: WwEconomy,
  ) {
    const sc = ww.scenario;
    const size = sc.maxRegionId() + 1;
    this.controller = new Uint16Array(size);
    this.supplyMilli = new Int32Array(size);
    this.encircled = new Uint8Array(size);
    const owners = sc.ownerByRegion();
    for (const r of sc.landRegions) this.controller[r.id] = owners[r.id];
    this.countryList = [null, ...sc.countries.map((tag, i) => new CountryLand(i + 1, tag))];
    sc.ensureRegionTiles(mg.map());
    this.rebuildDerived();
  }

  get scenario() {
    return this.ww.scenario;
  }

  /** Spec §6.4: templates, generals, the 1936 order of battle. WorldWar.start calls it once, after economy.start. */
  start(): void {
    startLand(this);
    for (const cl of this.countries()) {
      const p = this.mg.playerBySmallID(cl.smallID);
      if (p.isPlayer()) p.setTroops(this.fieldMen(cl.smallID));
      this.dirtyLandCountries.add(cl.smallID);
    }
    for (const id of this.armies.keys()) this.dirtyArmies.add(id);
    for (const r of this.scenario.landRegions) this.dirtyLandRegions.add(r.id);
  }

  // ---- Clock (spec §2.2) ----

  tick(ticks: number): void {
    const prep = this.ww.inPrep(ticks);
    if (!prep) {
      const day = this.ww.day(ticks);
      if (day !== this.lastCommonDay) {
        this.lastCommonDay = day;
        this.dailyCommon();
      }
      const k = ticks - dayStartTick(day, this.ww.prepEndTick());
      if (k >= 0 && k < WW_DAY_SPLIT) {
        for (const side of this.sideListCache) {
          if (side.rep % WW_DAY_SPLIT === k) dailySupply(this, side.key);
        }
        for (let id = 1; id < this.countryList.length; id++) {
          if (id % WW_DAY_SPLIT === k) this.dailyCountry(id, day);
        }
      }
    }
    applyArmyOrders(this, ticks);
    stepMovement(this, ticks);
    if (!prep) stepBattles(this, ticks);
    stepRecovery(this);
    if (!prep) stepSpread(this);
  }

  private dailyCommon(): void {
    if (this.divisions.compact()) {
      this.statsCache.clear();
      this.regionRowsDirty = true;
    }
    // Techs may have changed overnight: every division's numbers again.
    this.statsCache.clear();
    this.tplCache.clear();
    for (const cl of this.countries()) cl.casualtiesToday = 0;
  }

  private dailyCountry(id: number, day: number): void {
    const cl = this.countryList[id];
    const p = this.mg.playerBySmallID(id);
    if (cl === null || cl === undefined || !p.isPlayer() || !p.isAlive() || this.ww.isCapitulated(id)) {
      return;
    }
    dailyFronts(this, id, day);
    dailySustain(this, id, day);
    dailyEntrench(this, id);
    if (p.type() !== PlayerType.Human || this.armiesOf(id).some((a) => a.auto)) {
      aiLandTick(this, id, day);
    }
    // Spec §13.2: the troop number mirrors the men in the field.
    p.setTroops(this.fieldMen(id));
    this.dirtyLandCountries.add(id);
  }

  // ---- Orders (spec §16.3; the same for humans and the AI) ----

  issue(smallID: number, order: WwOrder): WwOrderBlock | null {
    const cl = this.country(smallID);
    const p = this.mg.playerBySmallID(smallID);
    if (cl === null || !p.isPlayer() || !p.isAlive() || this.ww.isCapitulated(smallID)) return "dead";
    let block: WwOrderBlock | null;
    switch (order.kind) {
      case "template_save":
        block = saveTemplate(this, cl, order);
        break;
      case "template_delete":
        block = deleteTemplate(this, cl, order.template);
        break;
      case "train":
        block = queueTraining(this, cl, order);
        break;
      case "cancel_train":
        block = cancelTraining(this, cl, order.entry);
        break;
      case "deploy_now":
        block = deployNow(this, cl, order.entry);
        break;
      case "army_general":
        block = assignGeneral(this, cl, order.army, order.general);
        break;
      case "appoint_general":
        block = appointGeneral(this, cl);
        break;
      case "army_move":
      case "army_rail":
      case "army_sea":
      case "army_attack":
      case "army_front":
      case "army_plan":
      case "army_execute":
      case "army_halt":
      case "army_split":
      case "army_merge":
      case "army_disband":
      case "army_auto":
      case "paradrop":
        block = issueArmyOrder(this, cl, order);
        break;
      default:
        return "bad_index";
    }
    if (block === null) this.dirtyLandCountries.add(smallID);
    return block;
  }

  // ---- Sides (spec §7.1: the faction, or the country alone) ----

  sideOf(smallID: number): number {
    return this.sideOfId[smallID] ?? smallID;
  }

  sameSide(a: number, b: number): boolean {
    return a > 0 && b > 0 && (a === b || this.sideOf(a) === this.sideOf(b));
  }

  sideMembers(side: number): readonly number[] {
    return this.sideListCache.find((s) => s.key === side)?.members ?? [];
  }

  sideList(): readonly Side[] {
    return this.sideListCache;
  }

  /** At war and past the preparation minute (P0 canFight). */
  isEnemy(a: number, b: number): boolean {
    return a > 0 && b > 0 && this.ww.canFight(a, b, this.mg.ticks());
  }

  /** Spec §7.1: own, same side, or an enemy's region (to attack or take). */
  canEnter(smallID: number, region: number): boolean {
    const c = this.controller[region] ?? 0;
    return c !== 0 && (this.sameSide(c, smallID) || this.isEnemy(smallID, c));
  }

  // ---- Control (decision 9: the only way it changes) ----

  /**
   * Hands `region` to `by` this tick, its victory-point tile with it
   * (Review Focus 1). The rest of its tiles follow by the spread (Task 4).
   */
  setController(region: number, by: number, tick: number, notify = true): void {
    const old = this.controller[region];
    if (old === by) return;
    this.controller[region] = by;
    this.controlChanges++;
    const r = this.scenario.region(region)!;
    const vpTile = this.scenario.vpTile(r);
    const p = this.mg.playerBySmallID(by);
    if (p.isPlayer() && this.mg.map().isLand(vpTile) && this.mg.ownerID(vpTile) !== by) {
      p.conquer(vpTile);
    }
    this.dirtyLandRegions.add(region);
    this.econ.markRegionDirty(region);
    // A capitulation hands over dozens of regions at once: no message each.
    if (notify && r.vp > 0) {
      this.message(by, "ww1936_region_taken", { region: r.id });
      if (old !== 0) this.message(old, "ww1936_region_lost", { region: r.id });
    }
    void tick;
  }

  // ---- Divisions ----

  /**
   * A new division in `army` at `region` (spec §5.1, §6.4). Men and
   * equipment come with it: the caller drew them (Stock.ts).
   */
  spawnDivision(owner: number, template: number, region: number, army: Army, men: number, equip: ArrayLike<number>, orgMilli: number): number {
    const id = this.nextDivisionId++;
    const r = this.divisions.add(id, owner, template, army.id, region);
    this.divisions.men[r] = men;
    this.divisions.org[r] = orgMilli;
    this.divisions.equipOf(r).set(Array.from(equip));
    army.divisions.push(id);
    this.regionRowsDirty = true;
    this.dirtyArmies.add(army.id);
    return id;
  }

  /**
   * Removes a division (spec §5.3 disband after its refund, §9.6 destroyed,
   * §5.5 capitulation). Its army loses it; an empty army goes.
   */
  killDivision(row: number, destroyed: boolean): void {
    const t = this.divisions;
    const id = t.id[row];
    if (id === 0) return;
    if (destroyed) {
      const cl = this.country(t.owner[row]);
      if (cl !== null) cl.casualtiesToday += t.men[row];
    }
    const army = this.armies.get(t.army[row]);
    if (army !== undefined) {
      army.divisions = army.divisions.filter((d) => d !== id);
      this.dirtyArmies.add(army.id);
      if (army.divisions.length === 0) this.removeArmy(army.id);
    }
    const b = this.battles.get(t.battle[row]);
    if (b !== undefined) {
      b.attackers = b.attackers.filter((d) => d !== id);
      b.defenders = b.defenders.filter((d) => d !== id);
      b.attLine = b.attLine.filter((d) => d !== id);
      b.defLine = b.defLine.filter((d) => d !== id);
    }
    t.kill(row);
    this.statsCache.delete(id);
    this.regionRowsDirty = true;
  }

  setDivisionRegion(row: number, region: number): void {
    if (this.divisions.region[row] === region) return;
    this.divisions.region[row] = region;
    this.regionRowsDirty = true;
    this.dirtyArmies.add(this.divisions.army[row]);
  }

  /** Live rows in a region, in row (= id) order. */
  rowsIn(region: number): readonly number[] {
    if (this.regionRowsDirty) {
      const out: number[][] = [];
      const t = this.divisions;
      for (let r = 0; r < t.rows; r++) {
        if (t.id[r] === 0) continue;
        (out[t.region[r]] ??= []).push(r);
      }
      this.regionRows = out;
      this.regionRowsDirty = false;
    }
    return this.regionRows[region] ?? [];
  }

  templateOf(owner: number, template: number): LandTemplate | null {
    return this.country(owner)?.template(template) ?? null;
  }

  /** Full-strength numbers at the division's generations (org max, width, speed, supply use). */
  baseStatsOf(row: number): DivisionStats {
    return this.statsEntry(row).base;
  }

  /** Numbers at its men and equipment now (attack, defence, HP). */
  statsOf(row: number): DivisionStats {
    return this.statsEntry(row).eff;
  }

  /** Call after changing a division's men, equipment or template. */
  touchDivision(row: number): void {
    this.statsCache.delete(this.divisions.id[row]);
    this.dirtyArmies.add(this.divisions.army[row]);
  }

  /** Maximum org with P3's orgPct (spec §9.8). */
  orgMaxOf(row: number): number {
    const ce = this.econ.country(this.divisions.owner[row]);
    const pct = ce === null ? 0 : ce.tech.orgPct;
    return Math.floor((this.baseStatsOf(row).orgMilli * (100 + pct)) / 100);
  }

  private statsEntry(row: number): StatsEntry {
    const t = this.divisions;
    const id = t.id[row];
    const hit = this.statsCache.get(id);
    if (hit !== undefined) return hit;
    const owner = t.owner[row];
    const tpl = this.templateOf(owner, t.template[row]);
    const ce = this.econ.country(owner)!;
    const equip = t.equipOf(row);
    const base = tpl === null
      ? templateStats({ name: "", battalions: [], support: [] }, ce.tech, genMixOf(equip))
      : templateStats(tpl.def, ce.tech, genMixOf(equip));
    const entry = { base, eff: divisionStats(base, equip, t.men[row]) };
    this.statsCache.set(id, entry);
    return entry;
  }

  /** Template numbers at generation 1 (screens, AI), cached per country and template. */
  templateStatsOf(owner: number, template: number): DivisionStats | null {
    const key = `${owner}:${template}`;
    const hit = this.tplCache.get(key);
    if (hit !== undefined) return hit;
    const tpl = this.templateOf(owner, template);
    const ce = this.econ.country(owner);
    if (tpl === null || ce === null) return null;
    const s = templateStats(tpl.def, ce.tech, genMixOf(new Array<number>(GROUND_SLOTS).fill(0)));
    this.tplCache.set(key, s);
    return s;
  }

  strengthOf(row: number): number {
    return strengthPm(this.baseStatsOf(row), this.divisions.equipOf(row), this.divisions.men[row]);
  }

  // ---- Armies (spec §6.1) ----

  newArmy(owner: number): Army | null {
    const cl = this.country(owner);
    if (cl === null || this.armiesOf(owner).length >= WW_MAX_ARMIES) return null;
    const a: Army = {
      id: this.nextArmyId++,
      owner,
      nameNo: cl.nextArmyNo++,
      general: 0,
      divisions: [],
      order: idleOrder(),
      auto: false,
      planBp: 0,
      front: [],
      path: [],
      pending: false,
      paraLastDay: -1,
    };
    this.armies.set(a.id, a);
    this.dirtyArmies.add(a.id);
    return a;
  }

  /** Spec §6.1: an army without divisions goes; its general is free again. */
  removeArmy(id: number): void {
    const a = this.armies.get(id);
    if (a === undefined) return;
    const g = this.country(a.owner)?.generals.find((x) => x.army === id);
    if (g !== undefined) g.army = 0;
    this.armies.delete(id);
    this.dirtyArmies.delete(id);
    this.removedArmyIds.push(id);
  }

  /** Moves a division to another army (split, merge, AI grouping). */
  moveToArmy(divisionId: number, army: Army): void {
    const r = this.divisions.rowOf(divisionId);
    if (r < 0) return;
    const old = this.armies.get(this.divisions.army[r]);
    if (old !== undefined && old !== army) {
      old.divisions = old.divisions.filter((d) => d !== divisionId);
      this.dirtyArmies.add(old.id);
      if (old.divisions.length === 0) this.removeArmy(old.id);
    }
    if (!army.divisions.includes(divisionId)) {
      army.divisions.push(divisionId);
      army.divisions.sort((a, b) => a - b);
    }
    this.divisions.army[r] = army.id;
    this.dirtyArmies.add(army.id);
  }

  armiesOf(smallID: number): Army[] {
    const out: Army[] = [];
    for (const a of this.armies.values()) if (a.owner === smallID) out.push(a);
    return out;
  }

  /** Spec §6.1: the region holding most of its divisions (ties: smaller id). */
  armyRegion(a: Army): number {
    const count = new Map<number, number>();
    for (const id of a.divisions) {
      const r = this.divisions.rowOf(id);
      if (r < 0) continue;
      const reg = this.divisions.region[r];
      count.set(reg, (count.get(reg) ?? 0) + 1);
    }
    let best = 0;
    let bestN = 0;
    for (const [reg, n] of count) {
      if (n > bestN || (n === bestN && reg < best)) {
        best = reg;
        bestN = n;
      }
    }
    return best;
  }

  // ---- Countries ----

  country(smallID: number): CountryLand | null {
    return this.countryList[smallID] ?? null;
  }

  countries(): CountryLand[] {
    return this.countryList.filter((c): c is CountryLand => c !== null);
  }

  fieldMen(smallID: number): number {
    const t = this.divisions;
    let n = 0;
    for (let r = 0; r < t.rows; r++) if (t.id[r] !== 0 && t.owner[r] === smallID) n += t.men[r];
    return n;
  }

  trainingMen(smallID: number): number {
    let n = 0;
    for (const x of this.country(smallID)?.trainees ?? []) n += x.men;
    return n;
  }

  /** P1's manpower ceiling counts these (spec §13.2). */
  armyMen(smallID: number): number {
    return this.fieldMen(smallID) + this.trainingMen(smallID);
  }

  day(): number {
    return this.ww.day(this.mg.ticks());
  }

  /** A message to the country's human player only (spec §15.8). */
  message(smallID: number, key: string, params: Record<string, string | number> = {}): void {
    const p = this.mg.playerBySmallID(smallID);
    if (!p.isPlayer() || p.type() !== PlayerType.Human) return;
    this.mg.displayMessage(`events_display.${key}`, MessageType.WORLD_WAR, p.id(), undefined, params);
  }

  /** AttackExecution / TransportShipExecution refused one (decision 7): tell a human once a game. */
  noteVanillaAttack(p: Player): void {
    const cl = this.country(p.smallID());
    if (cl === null || cl.warnedTileAttack || p.type() !== PlayerType.Human) return;
    cl.warnedTileAttack = true;
    this.message(p.smallID(), "ww1936_no_tile_attacks");
  }

  /** Spec §5.5, §12.5: a capitulated country's divisions, armies and training are gone. */
  removeCountryForces(smallID: number): void {
    const t = this.divisions;
    for (let r = 0; r < t.rows; r++) if (t.id[r] !== 0 && t.owner[r] === smallID) this.killDivision(r, false);
    for (const a of this.armiesOf(smallID)) this.removeArmy(a.id);
    const cl = this.country(smallID);
    if (cl !== null) {
      cl.training = [];
      cl.trainees = [];
      for (const g of cl.generals) g.army = 0;
    }
    this.dirtyLandCountries.add(smallID);
  }

  // ---- Change marks for the screens ----

  markArmy(id: number): void {
    this.dirtyArmies.add(id);
  }
  markBattle(region: number): void {
    this.dirtyBattles.add(region);
  }
  /** Adds a battle keeping `battles` in region order (Map order is iteration order). */
  addBattle(b: Battle): void {
    this.battles.set(b.region, b);
    const sorted = [...this.battles.entries()].sort((x, y) => x[0] - y[0]);
    this.battles.clear();
    for (const [k, v] of sorted) this.battles.set(k, v);
    this.dirtyBattles.add(b.region);
  }
  /** Sets a region's spread keeping `spreads` in region order. */
  setSpread(s: Spread): void {
    this.spreads.set(s.region, s);
    const sorted = [...this.spreads.entries()].sort((x, y) => x[0] - y[0]);
    this.spreads.clear();
    for (const [k, v] of sorted) this.spreads.set(k, v);
  }
  endBattle(region: number): void {
    if (this.battles.delete(region)) this.endedBattleIds.push(region);
  }
  markRegion(region: number): void {
    this.dirtyLandRegions.add(region);
  }
  markCountry(smallID: number): void {
    this.dirtyLandCountries.add(smallID);
  }

  // ---- Screens (spec §16.4) ----

  takeData(): WwLandData | null {
    if (
      this.dirtyArmies.size === 0 && this.battles.size === 0 && this.dirtyLandRegions.size === 0 &&
      this.dirtyLandCountries.size === 0 && this.removedArmyIds.length === 0 && this.endedBattleIds.length === 0
    ) {
      return null;
    }
    const asc = (s: Iterable<number>) => [...s].sort((a, b) => a - b);
    const d: WwLandData = {
      armies: [],
      removedArmies: asc(this.removedArmyIds),
      battles: [],
      endedBattles: asc(this.endedBattleIds),
      regions: [],
      countries: [],
      details: [],
    };
    for (const id of asc(this.dirtyArmies)) {
      const a = this.armies.get(id);
      if (a !== undefined) d.armies.push(this.armySummary(a));
    }
    // Spec §16.4: a running battle every tick.
    for (const region of asc(this.battles.keys())) d.battles.push(this.battleSummary(this.battles.get(region)!));
    for (const r of asc(this.dirtyLandRegions)) d.regions.push(this.regionData(r));
    for (const id of asc(this.dirtyLandCountries)) {
      const cl = this.country(id);
      if (cl === null) continue;
      d.countries.push(this.countrySummary(cl));
      const p = this.mg.playerBySmallID(id);
      if (p.isPlayer() && p.type() === PlayerType.Human) d.details.push(this.countryDetail(cl));
    }
    this.dirtyArmies.clear();
    this.dirtyBattles.clear();
    this.dirtyLandRegions.clear();
    this.dirtyLandCountries.clear();
    this.removedArmyIds = [];
    this.endedBattleIds = [];
    return d;
  }

  armySummary(a: Army): ArmySummary { /* below */ }
  battleSummary(b: Battle): BattleSummary { /* below */ }
  regionData(region: number): RegionLandData { /* below */ }
  countrySummary(cl: CountryLand): CountryLandSummary { /* below */ }
  countryDetail(cl: CountryLand): CountryLandDetail { /* below */ }

  // ---- Snapshot, hash ----

  hash(): number { /* below */ }
  snapshot(): WwLandState { /* below */ }
  restoreState(s: WwLandState): void { /* below */ }

  /** Sides, caches, the spread orders. The constructor and restoreState call it. */
  rebuildDerived(): void {
    const n = this.countryList.length;
    this.sideOfId = new Array<number>(n).fill(0);
    const byKey = new Map<number, Side>();
    for (let id = 1; id < n; id++) {
      const f = this.ww.factionOf(id);
      const key = f === null ? id : 100_000 + FACTION_IDS.indexOf(f);
      this.sideOfId[id] = key;
      let s = byKey.get(key);
      if (s === undefined) byKey.set(key, (s = { key, rep: id, members: [] }));
      s.members.push(id);
    }
    this.sideListCache = [...byKey.values()].sort((a, b) => a.rep - b.rep);
    this.statsCache.clear();
    this.tplCache.clear();
    this.regionRowsDirty = true;
    this.divisions.rebuildIndex();
    this.spreadOrders.clear(); // Task 4 rebuilds an order on its first use
  }

  /** P3 hook (P3 spec §17.3): factions changed, sides again. */
  onFactionsChanged(): void {
    this.rebuildDerived();
  }
}
```

화면 데이터 함수의 본문(위 `/* below */`)은 다음을 그대로 계산한다:

- `armySummary(a)`: 사단마다 `statsOf`를 `addToSide`로 더한 `sum`(조직력은 지금 조직력), `strengthPm` = 사단 `strengthOf`의 평균(내림), `orgPm` = Σ조직력 × 1000 / Σ`orgMaxOf`, `state` 비트(이동 중인 사단이 있으면 `ARMY_MOVING`, 그 사단의 방식에 따라 `ARMY_RAIL`/`ARMY_SEA`/`ARMY_RETREAT`, `battle ≠ 0`인 사단이 있으면 `ARMY_BATTLE`, 포위된 사단이 있으면 `ARMY_ENCIRCLED`, `order.executing`이면 `ARMY_EXECUTING`, `auto`면 `ARMY_AUTO`, 공수 명령이면 `ARMY_PARA`), `region` = `armyRegion(a)`, `move` = 움직이는 사단 중 `prog × 1000 / need`가 가장 작은 사단의 `{from: region, to: next, progPm, stepPm: step × 1000 / need}`(없으면 null), `mine` = 주인이 사람이면 `{order, front, planBp, auto, path, supplyPm: 사단 평균, etaTicks: 그 사단의 ceil((need − prog) / step)}`, 아니면 null.
- `battleSummary(b)`: 대표 나라, 전선/예비 수(`attLine.length`, `attackers.length − attLine.length`), 폭(`combatWidth(landOf, directions)`, 양쪽 전선 폭 합), 방향(공격 사단이 있는 서로 다른 주 수, 공수는 1), `progressPm` = `lostOrgMilli × 1000 / max(1, startOrgMilli)`, 양쪽 조직력‰(지금 / 최대), 전력‰(평균).
- `regionData(r)`: 통제국, `supplyMilli[r]`, 포위, 그 주 사단 수 상위 3개 나라(수 큰 순, 같으면 작은 smallID).
- `countrySummary(cl)`: 사단 수, `fieldMen`, `trainingMen`, `casualtiesToday`, 그 나라 사단이 낀 전투 수, 포위된 군 수.
- `countryDetail(cl)`: 편제(쓰는 사단 수 포함), 훈련 항목(대기 사유: 인력이 모자라 시작 못 한 남은 수가 있으면 `"manpower"`, 날은 찼고 장비 50% 미만인 훈련생이 있으면 `"equipment"`), 장군(쓸 수 있는 날 `from`/`until`을 `GENERALS`에서), 그 나라 사단 기록.
- `hash()`: spec §16.5 — 나라마다(smallID 순) 사단 수·인원 합·조직력 합, 전투 수와 `lostOrgMilli` 합, `controlChanges`, 군마다(번호 순) `armyRegion` 합, 번짐마다 `done` 합. `Math.imul(h, 31)` 누적, `>>> 0`.
- `snapshot()`: 모든 필드를 위 스키마로(군·전투·번짐은 번호 순, 편제는 `def`를 `name`/`battalions`/`support`로 펼친다). `restoreState`: 대입, `this.divisions.restore`, 그다음 `rebuildDerived()`, 모든 군·주·나라를 "바뀜"으로.

// land/Movement.ts (Task 3)
export function stepMovement(land: WwLand, tick: number): void {}
/** Starts division `row` on the next edge toward its dest (or stops it). */
export function startNextHop(land: WwLand, row: number, tick: number): void {}
/** Ticks for an army to walk `path` at its slowest division's pace (UI ETA, AI). */
export function pathTicks(land: WwLand, army: Army, path: readonly number[]): number { return 0; }
/** Shortest region path (Dijkstra, spec §7.3) for `owner`'s army over regions `pass` accepts; [] when none. */
export function findPath(land: WwLand, army: Army, from: number, to: number, pass: (region: number) => boolean): number[] { return []; }
/** Stops a division where it stands (finished here by the foundation; C and A call it). */
export function stopDivision(land: WwLand, row: number): void {
  const t = land.divisions;
  t.mode[row] = MOVE_NONE;
  t.prog[row] = 0;
  t.need[row] = 0;
  t.step[row] = 0;
  t.edge[row] = -1;
  t.dest[row] = t.region[row];
}
/** Starts a division straight into a neighbouring region (advance, retreat, walking into an empty enemy region). */
export function startHopInto(land: WwLand, row: number, region: number, mode: number): void {
  const t = land.divisions;
  const e = land.scenario.edgeBetween(t.region[row], region);
  if (e < 0) return;
  t.next[row] = region;
  t.edge[row] = e;
  t.prog[row] = 0;
  t.step[row] = 0;
  t.mode[row] = mode;
  t.dest[row] = region;
  t.need[row] = moveNeed(land.scenario.edge(e), land.scenario.landOf(region));
  land.markArmy(t.army[row]);
}

}

// land/Occupation.ts (Task 4)
/** Spec §7.7, §9.7, §12: `by` takes `region` now. Stub: the whole region at once. */
export function capture(land: WwLand, region: number, by: number, tick: number): void {
  land.setController(region, by, tick);
  const p = land.mg.playerBySmallID(by);
  if (!p.isPlayer()) return;
  for (const t of land.scenario.regionTiles(region)) if (land.mg.ownerID(t) !== by) p.conquer(t);
}
export function stepSpread(land: WwLand): void {}
/** Spec §12.5. Stub: control now, the tiles by P0's transfer queue (decision 8). */
export function transferOnCapitulation(land: WwLand, loser: number, victor: number): void {
  const tick = land.mg.ticks();
  for (const r of land.scenario.landRegions) {
    if (land.controller[r.id] === loser) land.setController(r.id, victor, tick);
  }
  const lp = land.mg.playerBySmallID(loser);
  if (lp.isPlayer() && victor !== 0) land.ww.queueTransfer(loser, victor, Uint32Array.from(lp.tiles()));
}

// land/Supply.ts (Task 5)

export function disbandDivision(land: WwLand, row: number): void {
  const ce = land.econ.country(land.divisions.owner[row]);
  if (ce !== null) returnDivisionEquip(ce, land.divisions.equipOf(row), land.divisions.men[row]);
  land.killDivision(row, false);
}

/** Spec §6.2: the general of an army, null when none. */

  return land.country(army.owner)?.generals.find((g) => g.id === army.general) ?? null;

- [ ] **Step 14: P1 경제와의 경계 `packages/engine/src/ww1936/economy/Stock.ts`** (spec §21.3, Global Constraints)

```ts
// The only paths between the P1 stockpile / manpower pool and the divisions
// (P2 spec §5.5). Generations never mix: a slot goes back to its own key.
import { EQUIPMENT_GENS, EquipmentType, equipKey } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/EconomyTypes";
import { GROUND_EQUIP_KEYS, GROUND_SLOTS, groundSlotOf } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/LandTypes";
import type { CountryEconomy } from "./WwEconomy";

/** Takes up to `n` of stock key `key`; returns how many it took. */
export function drawEquip(ce: CountryEconomy, key: number, n: number): number {
  const take = Math.max(0, Math.min(n, ce.stock[key]));
  ce.stock[key] -= take;
  return take;
}

export function returnEquip(ce: CountryEconomy, key: number, n: number): void {
  if (n > 0) ce.stock[key] += n;
}

/** All or nothing (spec §5.1: the men leave the pool at once). */
export function drawManpower(ce: CountryEconomy, men: number): boolean {
  if (men < 0 || ce.manpower < men) return false;
  ce.manpower -= men;
  return true;
}

export function returnManpower(ce: CountryEconomy, men: number): void {
  if (men > 0) ce.manpower += men;
}

/**
 * Fills `slots` (GROUND_SLOTS) toward `need` (EQUIPMENT_TYPES order) from
 * the stock, newest generation first (spec §5.1, §5.3); at most `cap` of
 * each type this call (Infinity: all that is missing).
 */
export function drawTemplateEquip(
  ce: CountryEconomy,
  slots: number[] | Int32Array,
  need: readonly number[],
  types: readonly EquipmentType[],
  cap: (needed: number) => number = (x) => x,
): void {
  types.forEach((type, i) => {
    let have = 0;
    for (let g = 1; g <= EQUIPMENT_GENS[type]; g++) {
      const s = groundSlotOf(equipKey(type, g));
      if (s >= 0) have += slots[s];
    }
    let want = Math.min(Math.max(0, need[i] - have), cap(need[i]));
    for (let g = EQUIPMENT_GENS[type]; g >= 1 && want > 0; g--) {
      const key = equipKey(type, g);
      const s = groundSlotOf(key);
      if (s < 0) continue;
      const got = drawEquip(ce, key, want);
      slots[s] += got;
      want -= got;
    }
  });
}

/** A division's or trainee's men and every equipment slot back to the pool and stock. */
export function returnDivisionEquip(ce: CountryEconomy, slots: ArrayLike<number>, men: number): void {
  for (let j = 0; j < GROUND_SLOTS; j++) returnEquip(ce, GROUND_EQUIP_KEYS[j], slots[j]);
  returnManpower(ce, men);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 15: 시작 편성 `packages/engine/src/ww1936/land/LandStartRun.ts`** (spec §4.5, §4.6, §6.4, 결정 14)

```ts
import { COUNTRIES } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/Countries";
import { EQUIPMENT_TYPES } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/EconomyTypes";
import { GROUND_SLOTS, idleOrder, WW_MAX_ARMY_DIVISIONS } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/LandTypes";
import { startArmy } from "@openfront/engine-lib/ww1936/EconomyStart";
import { GENERALS, generalServes, rosterOf, traitMask } from "@openfront/engine-lib/ww1936/Generals";
import { genericDivisions, landStartTechs, OobArmy, START_OOB, startTemplates } from "@openfront/engine-lib/ww1936/LandStart";
import { genMixOf, templateStats } from "@openfront/engine-lib/ww1936/TemplateStats";
import { drawManpower, drawTemplateEquip } from "../economy/Stock";
import type { CountryEconomy } from "../economy/WwEconomy";
import type { Army, CountryLand, GeneralState, LandTemplate, WwLand } from "./WwLand";

export function newGeneralState(rosterIndex: number): GeneralState {
  const g = GENERALS[rosterIndex];
  return {
    id: rosterIndex + 1, roster: rosterIndex, titleNo: 0,
    att: g.att, dfn: g.def, plan: g.plan, log: g.log, traits: traitMask(g.traits),
    xp: 0, level: 1, army: 0, use: [0, 0, 0, 0], terrainDays: [0, 0, 0, 0],
  };
}

/** The capital region if the country holds it, else its most valuable region (spec §11.1). */
export function capitalRegion(land: WwLand, smallID: number): number {
  const sc = land.scenario;
  const tag = sc.tagOfSmallId(smallID)!;
  const cap = sc.regionAt(sc.capitalTile(tag));
  if (land.controller[cap] === smallID) return cap;
  let best = 0;
  let bestVp = -1;
  for (const r of sc.landRegions) {
    if (land.controller[r.id] === smallID && r.vp > bestVp) {
      best = r.id;
      bestVp = r.vp;
    }
  }
  return best;
}

/** One full-strength division as far as manpower and stock allow; false when the men ran out. */
export function fieldDivision(land: WwLand, cl: CountryLand, ce: CountryEconomy, tpl: LandTemplate, region: number, army: Army): boolean {
  const s = templateStats(tpl.def, ce.tech, genMixOf(new Array<number>(GROUND_SLOTS).fill(0)));
  if (!drawManpower(ce, s.men)) return false;
  const slots = new Array<number>(GROUND_SLOTS).fill(0);
  drawTemplateEquip(ce, slots, s.need, EQUIPMENT_TYPES);
  const full = templateStats(tpl.def, ce.tech, genMixOf(slots));
  land.spawnDivision(cl.smallID, tpl.id, region, army, s.men, slots, full.orgMilli);
  return true;
}

export function startLand(land: WwLand): void {
  const sc = land.scenario;
  const historical = sc.data.historicalOob === true;
  const counts = new Map<number, number>();
  const plans: { cl: CountryLand; ce: CountryEconomy; oob: readonly OobArmy[] | null }[] = [];
  for (const cl of land.countries()) {
    const p = land.mg.playerBySmallID(cl.smallID);
    const ce = land.econ.country(cl.smallID);
    if (!p.isPlayer() || ce === null) continue;
    let civ = 0;
    for (const id of land.econ.regionsOf(cl.smallID)) civ += land.econ.civ[id];
    const dev = sc.devOf(cl.tag, civ);
    for (const t of landStartTechs(cl.tag, COUNTRIES[cl.tag].major, dev)) ce.addTech(t);
    for (const st of startTemplates(cl.tag, dev)) {
      cl.templates.push({ id: cl.nextTemplateId++, key: st.key, def: st.def });
    }
    for (const id of rosterOf(cl.tag)) cl.generals.push(newGeneralState(id - 1));
    const oob = historical ? (START_OOB[cl.tag] ?? null) : null;
    const first = land.templateStatsOf(cl.smallID, cl.templates[0].id)!;
    const army = startArmy(cl.tag, land.econ.recruitableWith(cl.smallID, ce.law().conscriptionPermille));
    counts.set(cl.smallID, oob === null ? genericDivisions(army, dev, first.men) : oob.reduce((n, a) => n + a.divisions.reduce((m, [, k]) => m + k, 0), 0));
    plans.push({ cl, ce, oob });
  }
  const day = land.day();
  for (const { cl, ce, oob } of plans) {
    const armies: Army[] = [];
    if (oob !== null) {
      for (const o of oob) {
        const region = o.regions.find((r) => land.controller[r] === cl.smallID) ?? capitalRegion(land, cl.smallID);
        const a = land.newArmy(cl.smallID);
        if (a === null) break;
        armies.push(a);
        for (const [key, n] of o.divisions) {
          const tpl = cl.templates.find((t) => t.key === key) ?? cl.templates[0];
          for (let i = 0; i < n && a.divisions.length < WW_MAX_ARMY_DIVISIONS; i++) {
            if (!fieldDivision(land, cl, ce, tpl, region, a)) break;
          }
        }
        if (o.planTargets !== undefined && o.planTargets.length > 0) {
          a.order = { ...idleOrder(), kind: "plan", targets: [...o.planTargets], stance: "balanced", executing: true };
          a.pending = true;
        }
        if (a.divisions.length === 0) land.removeArmy(a.id);
      }
    } else {
      const regions = startRegions(land, cl.smallID, counts);
      let left = counts.get(cl.smallID) ?? 0;
      for (let i = 0; left > 0 && regions.length > 0; i++) {
        const a = land.newArmy(cl.smallID);
        if (a === null) break;
        armies.push(a);
        const region = regions[i % regions.length];
        let ok = true;
        for (let k = 0; k < WW_MAX_ARMY_DIVISIONS && left > 0; k++, left--) {
          if (!fieldDivision(land, cl, ce, cl.templates[0], region, a)) {
            ok = false;
            break;
          }
        }
        if (a.divisions.length === 0) land.removeArmy(a.id);
        if (!ok) break;
      }
    }
    // Spec §6.4: the roster by skill sum to the armies by number.
    const free = cl.generals.filter((g) => g.roster >= 0 && generalServes(GENERALS[g.roster], day));
    let gi = 0;
    for (const a of armies) {
      if (!land.armies.has(a.id) || gi >= free.length) continue;
      a.general = free[gi].id;
      free[gi].army = a.id;
      gi++;
    }
  }
}

/** Spec §6.4 "그 밖": the capital, then a border region per neighbour, most divisions first. */
function startRegions(land: WwLand, smallID: number, counts: ReadonlyMap<number, number>): number[] {
  const out: number[] = [];
  const cap = capitalRegion(land, smallID);
  if (cap !== 0) out.push(cap);
  const neighbours = [...land.econ.countryNeighbours(smallID)].sort(
    (a, b) => (counts.get(b) ?? 0) - (counts.get(a) ?? 0) || a - b,
  );
  for (const n of neighbours) {
    let pick = 0;
    for (const id of land.econ.regionsOf(smallID)) {
      if (land.scenario.edgesOf(id).some((e) => land.controller[e.to] === n)) {
        pick = id;
        break;
      }
    }
    if (pick !== 0 && !out.includes(pick)) out.push(pick);
  }
  return out;
}
```

`econ.regionsOf`는 오름차순이므로 "작은 번호" 규칙이 저절로 맞는다. `countryNeighbours`는 P1이 타일 이웃으로 만든 나라 그래프다(해협 간선은 넣지 않는다 — 시작 배치는 땅으로만).

- [ ] **Step 16: P1 경제 연결과 다리 지우기** (결정 9, 10, 12, 13, 14)

`economy/WwEconomy.ts`:
- `fort`, `rail`, `hub` `Uint8Array`를 더한다. 생성자에서 `this.fort[id] = scenario.startFort(id)`, `this.rail[id] = scenario.startRail(id)`, `hub` 0. 스냅샷 키 `fort`, `rail`, `hub`(스키마에 `z.array(zInt())`), 복원도. `hash()`의 주 줄에 `+ this.fort[id] * 13 + this.rail[id] * 17 + this.hub[id] * 19`.
- `buildingArray(b: BuildingId): Uint8Array`(`civ`/`mil`/`dock`/`infra`/`fort`/`rail`, `supply_hub` → `hub`)를 더하고 `addBuilding`이 그것을 쓴다. `takeData`의 주 자료에 `fort`, `rail`, `hub`.
- `start()`: `p.setTroops(army)`, `fieldInfUnits`, `fieldInfGenSum`, `troopCap`, `updateCombatStats` 줄을 지운다. 대신

```ts
      const army = startArmy(ce.tag, recruitable);
      ce.stock = startStock(ce.tag, army, civ, mil);
      // P2 spec §6.4: the 1936 army's rifles go to the stock; land.start
      // fields the divisions from it and from the whole pool.
      ce.stock[equipKey("infantry_equipment", 1)] += Math.ceil(army / WW_MEN_PER_INF_EQUIPMENT);
      ce.manpower = recruitable;
```

- `stepCountry`: `applyCombatLosses`, `stepRecruit`, `updateCombatStats`를 지운다(`stepManpower`는 남김).
- `refreshControl`: 승점 타일을 읽던 줄을 `const c = this.ww.land.controller[r.id];`로(결정 9).
- `armyMen(p)`: 본문을 `return this.ww.land.armyMen(p.smallID());`로.
- 지운다: `noteCombatLoss`, `troopCap`, `combatStats`, `CountryEconomy.fieldInfUnits/fieldInfGenSum/lossMilli`(필드와 스냅샷 스키마), `Totals.troopCap/trainCap/recruited/attackPct/defensePct/speedPct`(타입, `emptyTotals`, `TotalsSchema`), `refreshTotals`의 `t.trainCap` 줄, `summary()`의 `combat`, `detail()`의 `fieldInfUnits/trainCap/recruited`. `summary().troopCap = this.ww.land.fieldMen(ce.smallID)`.

`MoveRules.ts`에 길 찾기를 더한다(엔진의 이동 갈래와 화면의 이동 미리보기가 같은 함수를 쓴다, spec §7.3):

```ts
/**
 * Dijkstra over the region graph (spec §7.3): regions `pass` accepts,
 * edge cost `cost(edgeIndex, toRegion)` in whole ticks. Ties: the smaller
 * region id settles first. [] when `to` is unreachable; [from] when equal.
 */
export function shortestPath(
  scenario: ScenarioIndex,
  from: number,
  to: number,
  pass: (region: number) => boolean,
  cost: (edge: number, to: number) => number,
): number[] {
  if (from === to) return [from];
  const dist = new Map<number, number>([[from, 0]]);
  const prev = new Map<number, number>();
  const done = new Set<number>();
  // A sorted frontier is enough for ~1,300 regions (binary heap if Task 11 measures a need).
  const open: number[] = [from];
  while (open.length > 0) {
    let bi = 0;
    for (let i = 1; i < open.length; i++) {
      const a = dist.get(open[i])!;
      const b = dist.get(open[bi])!;
      if (a < b || (a === b && open[i] < open[bi])) bi = i;
    }
    const u = open.splice(bi, 1)[0];
    if (u === to) break;
    done.add(u);
    for (const e of scenario.edgesOf(u)) {
      if (done.has(e.to) || (e.to !== to && !pass(e.to))) continue;
      const nd = dist.get(u)! + Math.max(1, cost(e.edge, e.to));
      const old = dist.get(e.to);
      if (old === undefined || nd < old || (nd === old && u < prev.get(e.to)!)) {
        if (old === undefined) open.push(e.to);
        dist.set(e.to, nd);
        prev.set(e.to, u);
      }
    }
  }
  if (!prev.has(to)) return [];
  const path = [to];
  while (path[path.length - 1] !== from) path.push(prev.get(path[path.length - 1])!);
  return path.reverse();
}
```

(`to`는 `pass`가 거절해도 마지막 칸으로는 받는다: "적 주와 맞닿은 곳까지 가서 공격"을 부르는 쪽이 정한다.)

`packages/engine-lib/src/ww1936/FrontRules.ts` (새, 스텁 — 본문 Task 3; 화면이 전선 미리보기에 쓴다):

```ts
export interface FrontWorld {
  scenario: ScenarioIndex;
  controller: ArrayLike<number>;
  /** a and b on the same side (spec §7.1). */
  sameSide(a: number, b: number): boolean;
  /** a may fight b now. */
  isEnemy(a: number, b: number): boolean;
}
/** Spec §8.1: our side's regions touching `enemy` (0: any enemy of `me`), ascending. */
export function borderRegionsOf(w: FrontWorld, me: number, enemy: number): number[] { return []; }
/** Spec §8.1: BFS over the border graph from `anchor`, neighbours by id, up to `count`. */
export function frontOf(w: FrontWorld, me: number, enemy: number, anchor: number, count: number): number[] { return []; }
/** Spec §8.1: ceil(divisions / 3). */
export function frontSize(divisions: number): number { return Math.max(1, Math.ceil(divisions / 3)); }
/** Spec §8.1: largest-remainder split of `n` divisions over `weights` (ties: smaller index). */
export function splitByWeight(n: number, weights: readonly number[]): number[] { return weights.map(() => 0); }
```

`src/client/view/WwLandView.ts` (완성):

```ts
import { ArmySummary, BattleSummary, CountryLandDetail, CountryLandSummary, RegionLandData, WwLandData } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/LandTypes";
import { ScenarioIndex } from "@openfront/engine-lib/ww1936/ScenarioIndex";

/**
 * World War 1936 P2 (spec §16.4): the screens' copy of the land war, merged
 * from WwLand updates. Render passes read only this (spec §21.3).
 */
export class WwLandView {
  readonly armies = new Map<number, ArmySummary>();
  readonly battles = new Map<number, BattleSummary>();
  readonly regions = new Map<number, RegionLandData>();
  readonly countries = new Map<number, CountryLandSummary>();
  readonly details = new Map<number, CountryLandDetail>();
  readonly controller: Uint16Array;
  /** Tick each army's summary arrived (token interpolation). */
  private readonly armyTick = new Map<number, number>();
  version = 0;

  constructor(readonly scenario: ScenarioIndex) {
    this.controller = new Uint16Array(scenario.maxRegionId() + 1);
    const owners = scenario.ownerByRegion();
    for (const r of scenario.landRegions) this.controller[r.id] = owners[r.id];
  }

  apply(d: WwLandData, tick: number): void {
    for (const a of d.armies) {
      this.armies.set(a.id, a);
      this.armyTick.set(a.id, tick);
    }
    for (const id of d.removedArmies) {
      this.armies.delete(id);
      this.armyTick.delete(id);
    }
    for (const b of d.battles) this.battles.set(b.region, b);
    for (const r of d.endedBattles) this.battles.delete(r);
    for (const r of d.regions) {
      this.regions.set(r.id, r);
      this.controller[r.id] = r.controller;
    }
    for (const c of d.countries) this.countries.set(c.smallID, c);
    for (const x of d.details) this.details.set(x.smallID, x);
    this.version++;
  }

  armiesOf(smallID: number): ArmySummary[] {
    return [...this.armies.values()].filter((a) => a.owner === smallID).sort((a, b) => a.id - b.id);
  }

  /** Armies whose token stands in `region`, by id. */
  armiesAt(region: number): ArmySummary[] {
    return [...this.armies.values()].filter((a) => a.region === region).sort((a, b) => a.id - b.id);
  }

  /** Token position in tiles at `tick` (fractional): between the two centers while it moves. */
  tokenXY(a: ArmySummary, tick: number): [number, number] {
    const w = this.scenario.width;
    const at = (region: number): [number, number] => {
      const t = this.scenario.centerTile(region);
      return [t % w, Math.floor(t / w)];
    };
    if (a.move === null) return at(a.region);
    const since = Math.max(0, tick - (this.armyTick.get(a.id) ?? tick));
    const p = Math.min(1000, a.move.progPm + a.move.stepPm * since) / 1000;
    const [x0, y0] = at(a.move.from);
    const [x1, y1] = at(a.move.to);
    return [x0 + (x1 - x0) * p, y0 + (y1 - y0) * p];
  }
}
```

`src/client/hud/ww1936/LandSelection.ts` (완성, 결정 25):

```ts
import { Stance } from "@openfront/engine-api/ww1936/LandTypes";
import { GameEvent } from "@openfront/shared/EventBus";

/** What a map tap means now (spec §15.3). */
export type LandMode = "none" | "move" | "attack" | "front" | "plan" | "rail" | "sea" | "paradrop";
export type WwArmyTab = "armies" | "training" | "templates" | "generals";

/** The chosen army and the tap flow's draft; shared by the controller, the card and the passes. */
export class WwLandSelection {
  army: number | null = null;
  mode: LandMode = "none";
  /** First tap of "tap → confirm tap": the region waiting for its second tap. */
  pending: number | null = null;
  /** Plan draft (spec §15.3-5). */
  targets: number[] = [];
  stance: Stance = "balanced";
  version = 0;

  select(army: number | null): void {
    this.army = army;
    this.mode = "none";
    this.pending = null;
    this.targets = [];
    this.version++;
  }

  setMode(mode: LandMode): void {
    this.mode = mode;
    this.pending = null;
    this.version++;
  }
}

const selections = new WeakMap<object, WwLandSelection>();
/** One selection per game view. */
export function landSelectionOf(game: object): WwLandSelection {
  let s = selections.get(game);
  if (s === undefined) selections.set(game, (s = new WwLandSelection()));
  return s;
}

export class WwSelectArmyEvent implements GameEvent {
  constructor(readonly army: number | null) {}
}
export class WwLandModeEvent implements GameEvent {
  constructor(readonly mode: LandMode) {}
}
export class OpenWwArmyEvent implements GameEvent {
  constructor(readonly tab: WwArmyTab = "armies") {}
}
export class OpenWwBattleEvent implements GameEvent {
  constructor(readonly region: number) {}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 20: 테스트 유틸** (`tests/ww1936/util.ts`에 더한다. 모든 갈래가 쓴다)

```ts
/** The land war of a World War game. */
export function land(game: Game): WwLand {
  return game.worldWar()!.land;
}

/** Hands a region to `tag` now, the way a won battle does (Occupation.capture). */
export function takeRegion(game: Game, region: number, tag: CountryTag): void {
  capture(land(game), region, byTag(game, tag).smallID(), game.ticks());
}

/** Removes every division and army (a blank board for a numeric test). Men and equipment do not come back. */
export function clearLand(game: Game): void {
  const l = land(game);
  for (let r = 0; r < l.divisions.rows; r++) if (l.divisions.alive(r)) l.killDivision(r, false);
}

/**
 * `n` full-strength divisions of `tag`'s template number `template` (1:
 * its first 1936 template) in `region`, as one new army; returns the army.
 * Draws men and equipment like training; the test country needs both.
 */
export function placeArmy(game: Game, tag: CountryTag, region: number, n: number, template = 1): Army {
  const l = land(game);
  const id = byTag(game, tag).smallID();
  const cl = l.country(id)!;
  const ce = l.econ.country(id)!;
  const a = l.newArmy(id)!;
  const tpl = cl.template(template)!;
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    if (!fieldDivision(l, cl, ce, tpl, region, a)) throw new Error(`${tag} ran out of men`);
  }
  return a;
}

/** Steps past the preparation minute and declares `a` → `b` (a human `a`). */
export function warAfterPrep(runner: GameRunner, clientID: string, b: CountryTag): void {
  stepTo(runner, WW_PREP_TICKS + 1);
  step(runner, 1, [stamped(clientID, { type: "declare_war", target: byTag(runner.game, b).id() })]);
}
```

수치 테스트의 표준 판: `wwRunner({}, [{ clientID: HUMAN, country: "DEU" }, { clientID: "HUMAN002", country: "POL" }])` → `clearLand` → `placeArmy`. 독일·폴란드가 사람이라 AI 지상전이 돌지 않는다(Global Constraints). 프랑스 등 AI는 평시라 독일·폴란드 땅에 들어오지 못한다.

- "predictBattle orders like spec §9.10": 같은 사단 1 대 1(평지) → `ratioPm` 550~700(방어 승); 독일 7 대 폴란드 보병 9 × 5, 두 방향 → `ratioPm` 1100~1200, `rounds` 20~21(공격 승).

- "weather (spec §10)": `weatherCell(200, 4880) = 378`. 테스트가 `hash32(wcell, floor(day / 7)) % 100`을 직접 계산해 표와 맞는지 본다: 온대(Rome 4190/379) 7월 = `h < 15 ? rain : clear`; 대륙(Moscow 5570/417) 1941-12-15 = `h < 40 ? frozen : snow`, 4월 10일 진흙, 3월 25일 진흙, 3월 10일 눈, 10월 20일 진흙, 11월 20일 눈; 남반구(−3400, 온대) 7월 = 1월 칸; 사막 7월 = `h < 70 ? heat : clear`; 열대(1000) 8월 = `h < 60 ? rain : clear`; 극지(6500) 1월 = `h < 60 ? frozen : snow`. 같은 칸·같은 주 → 같은 값.
- "shortestPath": 테스트 지도에서 베를린(3) → 키예프(9), 모든 주 통과, 비용 = 간선 km → `[3, 6, 8, 9]` 또는 `[3, 4, 7, 9]` 중 km 합이 작은 쪽(`163+220+200 = 583` 대 `140+185+220 = 545` → `[3, 4, 7, 9]`); 통과 금지 주를 넣으면 돌아간다; 닿을 수 없으면 `[]`.

### Task 2: 지도 자료 — 간선·강·해협·세부 지형·철도·요새·항구(`scenario.json` v3) + P4 지도 고침 (갈래 D)

**Files:**
- Create: `map-generator/cmd/ww1936build/{land.go,land_test.go,seasplit.go,seasplit_test.go,islands.go,islands_test.go,seaedges.go,seaedges_test.go}`, `map-generator/scenarios/world1936/src/{rail1936.json,forts1936.json,islands1936.json,straits1936.json,seasplit1936.json}`
- Modify: `map-generator/cmd/ww1936build/{main.go,output.go,regions.go,seas.go,cities.go}`, `map-generator/{map_generator.go,regions.go,regions_test.go,main.go}`, `map-generator/scenarios/world1936/{fetch.sh,README.md,scenario.src.json}`, `map-generator/scenarios/world1936/src/vp_cities.json`, `resources/maps/world1936/*`, `CREDITS.md`, `tests/ww1936/{World1936Data,World1936Load}.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1의 v3 스키마(`ScenarioRegionSchema`의 `land/center/latC/wcell/rail/fort/port/island`, `ScenarioCountrySchema.dev`, `ScenarioDataSchema`의 `edges/seaEdges/straits/historicalOob/kmPerTile`, `ScenarioSeaZoneSchema.lake`), `weatherCell` 공식(Go로 같은 식을 쓴다), `ScenarioIndex`의 읽기 함수(테스트).

- [ ] **Step 1: 원본 자료 받기** — `fetch.sh`에 두 파일을 더한다(같은 `fetch <file> <url> <sha256>` 방식, 체크섬은 처음 받은 값을 적는다): Natural Earth `ne_10m_rivers_lake_centerlines.geojson`(P0과 같은 고정 커밋 `ca96624a…`의 `geojson/`), NASA NEO "Land Cover Classification (MODIS)"의 0.1° 격자(NEO 데이터셋 `MCD12C1_T1`, 가장 최근 연도 하나, CSV 또는 GeoTIFF 중 NEO가 0.1°로 주는 것). 받지 못하면 스크립트가 실패한다(spec §3.2-5: 조용히 평지로 두지 않는다). 받은 파일은 읽기만 한다.
- [ ] **Step 2: 주 번호 고정** — 지금 도구가 주 번호를 매기는 곳(`cmd/ww1936build/regions.go`)에서, 커밋된 `resources/maps/world1936/scenario.json`(v2)의 `(owner, name, 조각 순번)` → `id` 표를 먼저 읽고 같은 열쇠의 주에 같은 번호를 준다. 표에 없는 새 조각(Step 4의 섬)만 `max(기존 번호) + 1 = 1343`부터 `islands1936.json` 순서로. 테스트 `land_test.go` "existing region ids are kept": 다시 만든 주 1,265개(+ 섬)의 `id → (owner, name, vpTile)`가 커밋된 v2와 같다.
- [ ] **Step 3: 해역 떼어 내기 (P4 spec §3.1)** — `seasplit1936.json`에 P4 표의 11줄(새 번호 40150~40160, 이름·한국어, 원래 해역, 경위도 상자; 40160은 "에게해 상자 밖"). `seasplit.go`: 해역 배정 뒤, 원래 해역에 속한 물 타일 중 상자 안의 것을 새 해역으로. 갈라진 작은 조각은 가장 긴 경계를 맞댄 이웃 해역에(번호 동점은 작은 쪽). 20타일 미만 규칙(P0)은 그대로. 40062는 "Central Mediterranean / 중앙 지중해". 호수 40048(카스피해), 40051(슈피리어호), 40058(미시간호)에 `lake: true`. 도구가 새 해역마다 타일 수를 찍는다.

  - `map.bin`의 땅 타일이 약 300개 는다: 도구가 정확한 수를 찍고 Step 11이 테스트 기대값을 다시 잡는다.

  - `center`: 주의 땅 타일 중 무게중심에 가장 가까운 타일(동점은 작은 타일 번호). `km`: 두 `center`의 경위도(밀러 역투영, 도구의 `geo.go`)로 대권 거리, 반올림 정수(오프라인이라 부동소수 가능).
  - 해협: 서로 다른 땅덩이(해협을 빼고 이어진 주 묶음)에 있는 두 주의 가장 가까운 땅 타일이 물로 6타일 이내면 `[a, b, 0, km, 0, 1]`. 도구가 해협 목록을 찍는다. 기대(손으로 확인): 메시나, 보스포루스·다르다넬스, 외레순·대벨트, 케르치, 지브롤터, 팔크.
  - 정리 뒤(생성기 Step 9) 남지 않은 주가 낀 간선은 버린다.
- [ ] **Step 6: 강 (spec §3.2-3)** — NE 강 중심선을 지도 타일로 래스터화(밀러 투영, 선분마다 타일 순서로). 두 주의 경계 타일 쌍 중 강 픽셀에서 2타일(체비셰프) 안에 있는 것이 40% 이상이면 강 간선: 그 쌍들을 지나는 선의 가장 작은 `scalerank`가 4 이하면 큰 강(2), 아니면 작은 강(1). 도구가 큰 강 간선 수와 라인·비스와·도나우·볼가·드네프르·돈·양쯔·황허의 간선 수를 찍는다.
- [ ] **Step 7: 세부 지형 (spec §3.2-5)** — 주 안 땅 타일마다 MODIS 0.1° 칸의 분류를 읽어 다수결(동점은 작은 분류 번호): 숲(IGBP 1~5) → `forest`(위도 ±23.5° 안의 상록 활엽수림 2 → `jungle`), 영구 습지(11) → `marsh`, 나지(16) → `desert`, 그 밖은 v1 `terrain`(`plains`/`highland`→`hills`/`mountain`). 마지막으로 등급 10 이상 도시가 있고 땅 타일이 400 미만인 주는 `urban`. 도구가 지형별 주 수를 찍는다.

- [ ] **Step 9: 해역 간선 (P4 spec §3.3)** — `seaedges.go`: 4방향으로 맞닿은 물 타일 쌍이 3쌍 이상인 두 해역 → `[a, b, km, 0]`(km = 두 해역 중심 사이 대권 거리; 해역 중심 = 무게중심에 가장 가까운 물 타일). `straits1936.json` 7줄(`bosporus`, `gibraltar`, `oresund`, `messina`은 해협, `suez`, `panama`, `kiel`은 운하; `a`, `b`, `shore` 주 이름): 물로 이미 이어진 곳은 그 간선의 종류를 1로, 아니면 손 간선을 더한다(종류 1 또는 2). 날짜변경선: x = 0 열과 x = W − 1 열에서 같은 행의 물 타일이 3개 이상 맞닿는 해역 쌍 → 종류 3(km는 짧은 쪽). 호수는 간선을 갖지 않는다. 도구가 해협·운하·날짜변경선 간선을 찍는다.
- [ ] **Step 10: 생성기 `map-generator/regions.go`** — `scenario.src.json` v3의 새 필드를 `scenario.json`으로 옮기고 `version: 3`. 정리(30타일 미만 섬, 200타일 미만 못)로 사라진 주가 낀 땅 간선·`shore`를 지우고, `coastal`·`port`를 최종 타일로 다시 계산한다. `regions_test.go`에 "v3 fields are copied", "edges of a dropped region are removed", "kept islands survive the cleanup".
- [ ] **Step 11: 다시 만들기와 테스트** — README 순서대로 `bash scenarios/world1936/fetch.sh` → `go run ./cmd/ww1936build` → `go run -tags nodynamic . --maps=world1936`. linux/amd64 Go 1.27.2. 두 번 돌려 바이트가 같은지 본다. `CREDITS.md`에 NE 강(공개 도메인), NASA NEO MODIS(공개 도메인).

`map-generator/cmd/ww1936build/*_test.go` (Go): 간선이 대칭·정렬·중복 없음; 알려진 두 점의 대권 거리(파리–베를린 중심 약 880km ±5%); 강 규칙(합성 래스터: 경계 10쌍 중 4쌍이 강 2타일 안 → 강, 3쌍 → 아님; scalerank 3 → 2, 6 → 1); 해협 규칙(합성: 물 6타일 → 간선, 7타일 → 없음); 지형 다수결과 `urban` 규칙; 섬 도장의 BFS 순서; 해역 상자 떼어 내기와 작은 조각 붙이기; 날짜변경선 간선(합성 지도의 양 끝 열); 같은 입력 → 같은 출력.

- 기존 주 1..1342의 `name`·`owner`·`vpTile`이 v2 때와 같다(테스트 안에 v2의 이 세 값 해시를 고정해 둔다: v2 파일에서 한 번 계산해 숫자로 적는다).

`tests/ww1936/World1936Load.test.ts`: 땅 타일 수 기대값을 도구가 찍은 새 값으로.
